Mint-failure branches "fall back" to an auth path that was deleted, H15. Proctoring frontend grows unboundedly and drains CPU for the whole session, H16. Proctoring backend: blocking KEYS + full-payload reads every 5 min, retries run serially on the scheduler thread, multi-MB parses on request threads, H17. Dashboard snapshot can compute hundreds of MB on the request thread — heap-kill risk (+11 more)

### Community 458 - "🟢 LOW (16 findings → 10 distinct issues)"
Cohesion: 0.18
Nodes (11): L10. Face counter loads WASM from jsdelivr `@latest` + Google-hosted model at runtime, L1. Login-page prefetch always fails (username string into a Long path param) — pure wasted load, L2. Allotted-assessments list never refreshed — admin resets invisible until re-login, L3. `document.cookie` (cn_csrf) logged to the console on every successful mint in production, L4. `flushOneStudent`'s `@Transactional` bypassed by self-invocation — delete and re-insert run in two separate transactions, L5. Bulk-submit endpoints catch per-row errors inside one shared transaction — one bad row can roll back the whole upload after reporting success, L6. `processBatch` runs the full 6-phase pipeline for every mapping synchronously in one HTTP request, L7. Heartbeat endpoint trusts body-supplied IDs — any authenticated student can overwrite another's live position (+3 more)
